# Intent Radar Phase 2/3 requirement-level audit

Baseline: 2026-11-11, HEAD `03398f62a3c040e2220157fd7726d7c1233c6969` plus preserved source checkpoint.
The original restartable checklist P2-A–E/P3-A–E is expanded using every detailed acceptance criterion in the owner request. No separate original detailed master document was found. IDs remain stable; historical reports are preserved.

Each record includes expected behavior, implementation, backend/frontend, API, storage, historical evidence, new verification, live acceptance, status, cause, repair, blocker and priority. Initial statuses are explicitly unverified, not claims that missing features are implemented.

Verification rule: exact implementation plus the applicable customer interaction, persistence, authorization and required live evidence must be reviewed. A suite pass never automatically verifies a row. External gates remain separate.

Source-native IDs/URLs, source timestamps, retrieval time, provenance and review state are retained. AI interpretation is separate and heuristic, not purchase probability.<br>Backend: `app/intent_radar/classification.py`<br>Frontend: `dashboard/components/intent-radar/evidence-experience.tsx`<br>API: `signals`<br>Storage: `intent_signals; intent_monitor_signals` | Historical: Historical evidence retained; relevant current existing regression files: tests/unit/test_intent_radar.py; tests/unit/test_intent_radar_intelligence.py. Related files alone do not establish this criterion.<br>New: Explicit source/contract review plus current bounded backend/DB acceptance (data/artifacts/verification/intent-radar23-product-backend-closure.xml; data/artifacts/verification/intent-radar23-product-postgres-recovery.xml) and applicable real-route actions (data/artifacts/verification/intent-radar23-product-radar-final-results.json; data/artifacts/verification/intent-radar23-product-phase23-recovery-results.json; data/artifacts/verification/intent-radar23-product-product-pages-results.json). Exact new regression: tests/unit/test_intent_radar_product_audit.py::test_topical_noncommercial_content_does_not_get_high_intent (six cases).<br>Live/manual: Live transport/UI where applicable: data/artifacts/verification/intent-radar23-product-live-sources-final.log; data/artifacts/verification/intent-radar23-product-live-final-results.json. Pure authorization/fixture cases are isolated acceptance, not vendor consent. | **VERIFIED**<br>Cause: No remaining defect in the assessed bounded contract.<br>Repair: Retain permanent regression and scope restrictions.<br>Blocker: None for this criterion.<br>Priority: P1 |

| ID / expected customer behavior | Actual / backend / frontend / API / database | Historical / new / live evidence | Status / cause / remediation / blocker / priority |

| **P3.5.01** — Problem relevance | Criterion: Problem relevance. INTENT_SCORE_V2 weighted evidence, expressed need, semantic topic alignment, inferred service fit, recency, excerpt quality/specificity and bounded review adjustment. ## Reproducible counts

Total requirements: **510**.

```json
{
  "BLOCKED BY EXTERNAL APPROVAL": 4,
  "IMPLEMENTED BUT NOT VERIFIED": 66,
  "MISSING": 30,
  "NOT APPLICABLE": 5,
  "PARTIALLY IMPLEMENTED": 50,
  "VERIFIED": 355
}
```

Closure = VERIFIED / (total − NOT APPLICABLE). Local functional completion uses the explicit `local_complete` review field; partial/missing and unperformed independent-evaluation rows receive no credit. External approval rows are excluded only from the local-function denominator and remain in the closure denominator. Neither metric is a test-pass rate. Counts and exact numerators/denominators: `INTENT_RADAR_PHASE_2_3_AUDIT_COUNTS.json`. Production readiness also requires commercial, quality and staging gates.
